`score_fast(a)`:
```
def self_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    N=1
    while N < 2*n-1: N*=2
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:2*n-1]
    # FFT numerical may have tiny negative; c nonnegative mathematically. Score uses max; negative irrelevant.
    return c
```
Score: `sum=a.sum(); score=2*n*c.max()/(sum*sum)`. Normalize internally or not. To avoid overflow, scale a to sum 1 before optimization. The score independent; if sum very small due to normalization, clamp.

Gradient:
```
def grad_smooth(a, c, beta):
    # c length 2n-1
    z=beta*c
    z-=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    # avoid overflow if beta*c max - min > 700; we subtract max so okay.
    p=e/e.sum()
    q=p[::-1]
    N=1
    while N < 3*n-2: N*=2
    Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fa,N)
    g=2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return g
```
Potential numerical: p sum 1, q, convolution h nonnegative? Should be. Gradient nonnegative.

Mirror update:
```
def mirror_step(a, g, lr):
    # a normalized positive
    # use log to avoid underflow
    loga = np.log(a + tiny) # but a may zeros; use log of clipped
    loga -= lr*g
    # subtract max before exp to scale
    a_new = np.exp(loga - loga.max())
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s<...: use fallback
    a_new /= s
    # ensure no zeros? keep zeros okay.
```
If a has zeros, log(-inf); we can set loga for zeros to max_log - 50 or -inf. But if zeros, gradient might be zero for some? To keep support, maybe initialize positive (all >0) and clip. Use `eps=1e-300`; log(eps) ~ -690. If lr*g > 700, underflow. We'll rescale. loga - lr*g could be -1000; exp underflow to 0. okay. But if all underflow? subtract max first.

Better: `w = a * np.exp(-lr*g)`; if min positive? For large lr*g, `np.exp` underflows; multiply: 1e-16 * exp(-100) still okay (1e-59). a min ~1e-? After many updates could underflow. Use log-domain:
```
loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
loga = loga - lr*g
m = loga.max()
a_new = np.exp(loga - m)
a_new = a_new / a_new.sum()
```
This maintains relative weights.

Let's implement a mirror descent with adaptive lr but not too many extra FFTs:
- Maintain current c and score.
- For each iteration, compute grad from current c.
- Try update. To evaluate, compute c_new at top of next iteration. If score_new >= score and no big progress, reduce lr, revert to previous a/c? We need store previous. If worsen, we can revert and continue with smaller lr without computing extra? But the already computed c_new is waste. That's okay.
Pseudo:
```
a_best=a; score_best=score; a_cur=a; c_cur=c_cur
lr = n / 1000  #? maybe 5
no_improve=0
for it:
   grad=compute_grad(c_cur,beta)
   loga=np.log(a_cur+eps)-lr*grad
   a_try=normalize(loga)
   c_try=self_conv(a_try)
   score_try=...
   if score_try < score_cur - 1e-15: accept; if < best... lr*=1.05; no_improve=0
   else:
      # maybe accept as random walk? reduce lr*0.7; if lr too small, add noise
      lr*=0.8
      if score_try < score_best: best? no, if worse than cur but maybe useful? reject.
      no_improve++
      if no_improve>limit: reset a_cur = best + noise, lr=..., beta...
```
This computes one candidate convolution per accepted/rejected iteration. Gradient uses previous c. Good.

Let's design a practical search that likely finds good sequence:
1. Generate several base sequences:
   - central binomial `comb(2i,i)/4^i` (same as sqrt power).
   - one-sided power `(i+1)^-alpha` for alpha grid 0.3-0.7.
   - symmetric gaussian? maybe.
   - height_sequence_1 if available.
   Normalize and evaluate official; keep best.
2. For best sequences, run local optimizer:
   - Use a **smooth maximum** objective with log-sum-exp and mirror descent with adaptive lr and β increasing.
   - Periodically run a "peak shaving" block: select top active lags, compute gradient, take a larger step with line search.
   - Add random restarts/noise with survival of best.
3. Additionally, perform a **parameter-space Nelder-Mead** on low-dimensional perturbations of best sequence:
   - a_i = base_i * exp(∑ θ_r φ_r(i)) with φ_r = sin/cos or polynomial basis, with small bounds to maintain positivity. Evaluate score; maybe finds global shape better.
   - Use this to improve initial point for full-sequence optimizer.

But code complexity and runtime. Need ensure no errors.

Pseudo:
```
def normalize_log(loga):
    loga = np.nan_to_num(loga, nan=-800, neginf=-800, posinf=100)
    m=loga.max()
    w=np.exp(loga-m)
    s=w.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    return w/s
```

`fast_score(a, conv=None):`
```
if conv is None: conv=self_conv(a)
s=a.sum()
score=2*n*conv.max()/(s*s)
return score, conv
```
Because a normalized sum1, s=1; but due normalization, still.

`run_mirror(a, time_limit, beta_schedule, lr_init, rng)`:
- `a = a.copy()` normalized; best=a,best_score=fast_score.
- `lr = max(1.0, 0.1 / (grad_p?))` after first grad.
- For each major beta in schedule:
  - Run until time or no improve threshold.
  - Each iteration:
    - c=self_conv(a) (or maintain c from previous? We need c for gradient. To avoid recomputing after accept, we can compute at top and use for score. After update, next top computes new c. Good.)
    - score = ...; if score<best: update best.
    - p=softmax(beta*c)
    - G=...
    - gmax=G.max(); if not finite or 0 break.
    - if lr is None: lr=0.1/gmax; else adjust.
    - loga=np.log(a+1e-300) - lr*G
    - a_try=normalize_log(loga)
    - # For speed, don't line search; just accept a_try? But need maintain descent? Could accept and see next score; if score worsens a lot, revert and lr*=0.5.
    - a_next=a_try; c_next? At next loop, if score worse than current by > tolerance, reduce lr and keep old a (maybe recompute with smaller). To do this, we need c/score for a_try before replacing. Let's compute c_next temporarily? Then only one extra FFT (same as next loop but we already need). We can accept if score_next < score_cur + tol; else revert.
       - Compute c_next=self_conv(a_try); score_next.
       - If score_next < score_cur - 1e-12 or small random: accept, set a,c,score; adjust lr.
       - Else: lr *= 0.7; if failures>limit, reset.
    This doubles FFT: one for current c (top) and one for candidate. But current c from previous candidate could be stored; if accepted, next current c already available. If rejected, we need compute candidate each try. We can loop:
```
c = self_conv(a); score=...
while:
  G=grad(c)
  for attempt in range(max_attempts):
    a_try=...
    c_try=self_conv(a_try)
    score_try=...
    if score_try < score - tol or lr < min:
       a,c,score=a_try,c_try,score_try
       if score < best...
       lr*=1.05
       break
    lr*=0.5
  if attempts exhausted: reset lr...
```
This computes c_try (one FFT) plus grad from c (no FFT). No top self_conv needed. Good. Two FFTs? Gradient uses c already; FFT for h. So per attempt: gradient FFT (rfft q,a) + candidate self FFT. If accept, next iteration uses c as current, no self conv. If reject tries, extra candidate FFTs. Most accept if lr appropriate. Good.

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _fft_conv_self(a): ...
def _fast_score(a): ...
```

Details:

`_next_pow2(m)`:
```
N=1
while N < m: N <<=1
return N
```

`_self_conv_fft(a, N=None)`:
```
n=a.size
if N is None: N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
return c[:2*n-1]
```
For gradient we need N_grad >= 3n-2. Use same N for both to reuse F. Let N = next_pow2(3n-2). Self conv uses N (zero-padded sufficiently). Fine. For n=8192, N=32768.

`_grad_from_c(a,c,beta,N,Fa=None)`:
```
L=2*n-1
z=beta*np.maximum(c,0.0)
zmax=z.max()
e=np.exp(z-zmax)
p=e/e.sum()
q=p[::-1]
if Fa is None: Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
h=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fa,N)
g=2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return g
```
We can pass Fa to avoid rfft for gradient. But after candidate update, Fa current changes.

In polishing:
```
N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
def conv_and_fft(a):
   Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,N)[:L]
   return c, Fa
```
Then loop:
```

    # maybe check periodic official for a and best
    g = grad(a,c,beta,N,Fa)
    gmax=g.max()
    if not finite or gmax<=0: break
    if lr is None: lr = 0.05/gmax
    lr = min(max(lr, 1e-12), 5.0*n)
    # Try up to 8 halvings
    accepted=False
    for attempt in range(8):
       loga=np.log(a+1e-300) - lr*g
       a_try=_normalize_log(loga)
       c_try,Fa_try = conv_and_fft(a_try)
       score_try=2*n*c_try.max()  # sum1
       if np.isfinite(score_try) and (score_try < score - 1e-13 or (score_try < best_score + 1e-7 and attempt>4)): # accept if better or small
           a,c,Fa,score=a_try,c_try,Fa_try,score_try
           if score < best_score:
              best=a.copy(); best_score=score; stagnant=0
           else: stagnant+=1
           lr = min(lr*1.05, 5*n)
           accepted=True
           fail=0
           break
       lr *= 0.5
       if lr < 1e-8/n: break
    if not accepted:
       fail += 1
       if fail % 100 == 0 or stag large:
          # perturb from best
          noise = rng.normal(0, 0.1, n)
          a = best * np.exp(noise)
          a = a / a.sum()
          c,Fa = conv_and_fft(a)
          score = 2*n*c.max()
          lr = None
       # maybe every 1000 failures restart
    if stagnant > 50000:
       # stronger restart
       a = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.3, n))
       a /= a.sum()
       c,Fa = conv_and_fft(a)
       score=2*n*c.max()
       lr=None
       stagnant=0
    if time near deadline: break
```

Let's detail helpers.

`_make_sequence_from_log(loga)`:
```
loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
loga = np.nan_to_num(loga, nan=-100.0, neginf=-100.0, posinf=100.0)
loga = np.clip(loga, -700.0, 700.0)
m=loga.max()
a=np.exp(loga-m)
s=a.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
return a/s
```

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,1000)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return np.ones(len(a))
return a/s
```

`_fast_score_normalized(a, N=None)` returning score, c. Use sum not assume1.

`_grad(a,c,beta,N,opt Fa)`:
Need N same. If Fa not provided, compute.

`_polish_sequence(a, time_limit, rng, target_score=None)`:
- a = _normalize(a)
- n = a.size; L=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
- c,Fa = conv(self)
- score=...
- best = a.copy(); best_score=score
- last_best = time.monotonic(); last_any = time.monotonic(); lr=None; accept_count=0; iterations=0
- deadline = now + time_limit
- no_improve count
- while now < deadline:
   - if iterations % 20000 ==0 print optional (maybe inside propose to avoid too much)
   - c maybe already; if c None: compute
   - gmax = g.max(); if not finite break
   - if lr is None: lr=0.05/gmax
   - Adaptive lr: `lr_target = 0.05/gmax`; if lr < lr_target: lr = min(max(lr*1.1, 0.5*lr_target), 2*lr_target)? Simpler cap.
   - for attempt in 10:
       a_try = log update
       c_try,Fa_try=conv
       score_try = 2*n*c_try.max()/(a_try.sum()**2)
       if score_try < score * (1 - 1e-12) - 1e-15: accept
       else lr*=0.5
     if not accept:
        if no_accept > 5000: restart
   - if score < best_score: update; last_best=now
   - else if now-last_best>40: restart no density
   - if time-left <20 break
- Official check inside? We can accept official_score passed? In polish, don't call official to keep generic. In propose, after polish call official.

```
   sigma = 0.1
   a = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, n))
   a = _normalize(a)
   c,Fa=conv(a)
   score=...

```

Candidate generation details:
```
def _candidate_power(n, alpha, lam, gamma, rng=None):
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   arr = np.power(x+1.0, -alpha)
   if lam:
       t=x/(n-1)
       w=1.0 + lam*t  # increasing late
       # ensure nonnegative
       w=np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
       arr = arr * np.power(w, gamma)
   arr = np.clip(arr,0,1e100)
   if arr.sum()>0: arr/=arr.sum()
   return arr
```
But earlier increasing late may worsen; but random lam positive/negative.
Use several:
- pure alpha in [0.45,0.5,0.55]
- alpha=0.5, lam in [-0.5,0,0.5], gamma=1
- sine mod: base alpha=0.5 * (1 + a*sin(2πx)+b*sin(4πx)) positive clip.
- random log basis.
- central binomial: Use recurrence to avoid comb overflow:
`a[0]=1; for i: a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)`? Actually c_i = c_{i-1}*(2i-1)/(2i). For i=1:1? c0=1, c1=1/2: factor (1)/(2) yes. Use float.

Random log basis:
```
def _random_log_candidate(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   eps=1e-3
   phi=[]
   phi.append(np.ones(n))
   phi.append(x)
   phi.append(x*x)
   phi.append((x-0.5)**2)
   phi.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
   phi.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
   phi.append(np.sin(4*np.pi*x))
   phi.append(np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
   phi.append(1.0/np.sqrt(x+eps))
   phi.append(1.0/np.sqrt(1.0-x+eps))
   phi.append(np.exp(-x/0.1))
   phi.append(np.exp(-(1-x)/0.1))
   coeffs=rng.normal(size=len(phi), scale=0.7)
   loga=coeffs[0]
   for c,p in zip(coeffs,phi): loga += c*p
   loga = np.clip(loga, -50, 50)
```

Let's draft code in mind:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n

def _normalize_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != (a.size,): a=a.ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s
```
But if s=0 and size 0? not.

`_self_conv_fft(a, N=None)`:
```
n=a.size
L=2*n-1
if N is None: N=_next_pow2(L)
F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
return c[:L], F # maybe return F? for score only no F.
```
Need two variants. `_self_conv_and_fft(a,N)` returns c,Fa.

`_score_from_conv(c,a)`:
```
s=a.sum()
return float(2*a.size*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```

`_grad_from_p(a,p,N,Fa)`:
```
n=a.size; L=2*n-1
q=p[::-1]
Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,N)
h=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fa,N)
g=2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return g
```
Need ensure p length L.

`_p_from_c(c, top_frac)`:
```
cmax=np.max(c)
med=np.median(c)
thr = cmax - top_frac*max(1e-300, cmax-med)
active = c >= thr
cnt=active.sum()
if cnt ==0:
   active[int(np.argmax(c))]=True; cnt=1
p=np.zeros_like(c)
p[active]=1.0/cnt
return p
```
If all c equal, med=cmax, thr=cmax, active all. Good.

`_log_update(a, g, lr)`:
```
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) - lr*g
# clip loga to avoid overflow/underflow
loga=np.nan_to_num(loga, nan=-700, posinf=100, neginf=-700)
m=loga.max()
if not np.isfinite(m): return a.copy()
w=np.exp(loga-m)
s=w.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return a.copy()
return w/s
```

`_polish_sequence(a, time_limit, rng)` as above. Need prints maybe inside with iteration count. To avoid excessive prints, print at start/end.

In polish, use official? No.

```
def _polish_sequence(a, time_limit, rng):
    a=_normalize_array(a)
    n=a.size
    if n <2 or time_limit <=5:
       return a, _fast_score(a)[0]
    L=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    c,Fa=_self_conv_and_fft(a,N)
    score=_score_from_conv(c,a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    lr=None; top_frac=0.05; no_accept=0; stale=0
    last_best=time.monotonic(); last_start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    iter_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if score < best_score:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=score; last_best=time.monotonic()
       # restart conditions
       if not np.isfinite(score) or time.monotonic()-last_best > 60.0 or no_accept > 20000:
          a=best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n))
          a=_normalize_array(a)
          c,Fa=_self_conv_and_fft(a,N)
          score=_score_from_conv(c,a)
          lr=None; top_frac=0.05; no_accept=0
          iter_count=0
          continue
       p=_p_from_c(c, top_frac)
       g=_grad_from_p(a,p,N,Fa)
       gmax=np.max(g)
       if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0:
          break
       if lr is None:
          lr=0.02/gmax
       lr = float(np.clip(lr, 1e-12/n, 5.0*n))
       accepted=False
       for attempt in range(12):
          a_try=_log_update(a,g,lr)
          c_try,Fa_try=_self_conv_and_fft(a_try,N)
          score_try=_score_from_conv(c_try,a_try)
          if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < score - 1e-13:
             a=a_try; c=c_try; Fa=Fa_try; score=score_try
             if score < best_score:
                top_frac = max(0.005, top_frac*0.9)
             else:
                top_frac = min(0.2, top_frac*1.05)
             lr = min(lr*1.05, 5.0*n)
             accepted=True
             no_accept=0
             break
          lr *= 0.5
       if not accepted:
          no_accept += 1
          if no_accept % 1000 == 0:
              top_frac = min(0.5, top_frac*1.3)
          if no_accept > 20000:
              pass
       iter_count += 1
       if iter_count % 20000 == 0:
          print(f"    polish iter {iter_count} score {score:.6f} best {best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return best, best_score
```


`_candidate_power(n, alpha, lam, gamma, rng=None)`:
```
i=np.arange(n,float)
base=np.power(i+1.0, -alpha)
if abs(lam)>1e-8:
    x=i/(n-1) if n>1 else np.zeros(n)
    w=1.0 + lam*x
    w=np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    if gamma !=1:
       w=np.power(w, gamma)
    base=base*w
base=np.nan_to_num(base, nan=0, posinf=1000, neginf=0)
if base.sum()<=0: return None
return base/base.sum()
```

```
    loga += rng.normal(0.0, scale)*p

return np.ones(n)/n
```

```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
eps=1e-3
basis=[]
basis.append(np.ones(n))
basis.append(x)
basis.append(x*x)
basis.append((x-0.5)*(x-0.5))
basis.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
basis.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
basis.append(np.sin(4*np.pi*x))
basis.append(np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
basis.append(np.sin(6*np.pi*x))
basis.append(np.cos(6*np.pi*x))
basis.append(1.0/np.sqrt(x+eps))
basis.append(1.0/np.sqrt(1.0-x+eps))
basis.append(np.exp(-x/0.05))
basis.append(np.exp(-x/0.2))
basis.append(np.exp(-(1.0-x)/0.05))
basis.append(np.exp(-(1.0-x)/0.2))
return basis
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0, int(budget_s) - 10)
    n = 5000
    # If height_sequence length maybe use that? But we choose n.
    # If budget very small, return uniform or height.
    print(f"Starting proposal n={n}, budget={budget_s}")
    inits = _generate_initial_candidates(n, rng)
    top, best_fast = _select_top_candidates(inits, n, rng, 6)
    best_a = top[0]
    best_score = np.inf
    # official best init
    try: val=evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist()); if finite: best_score=val
    except: best_score=np.inf
    # Polish top candidates
    time_left = deadline - now
    fracs = [0.5,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.05]
    for i,arr in enumerate(top):
       if time.monotonic()+30 > deadline: break
       alloc = max(30, fracs[i]*time_left)
       if alloc > deadline - time.monotonic() - 20: alloc=deadline - time.monotonic() - 20
       print Polish i
       a_best, fast_score = _polish_sequence(arr, alloc, rng)
       # official
       val = _evaluate_safe(a_best)
       if val < best_score:
          best_score=val; best_a=a_best
       # also if fast_score < best? no need
    # final global polish with remaining on best_a
    rem = deadline - now - 20
    if rem > 20:
       a_best, fast_score = _polish_sequence(best_a, rem, rng)
       val=evaluate safe; update
    # final official check of best_a and maybe top[0][1]? Already.
    # ensure nonnegative finite
    return [float(max(0,min(1000,x))) for x in np.asarray(best_a).tolist()]
```

`_evaluate_safe`:
```
def _evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
    except Exception:
       val = np.inf
    if np.isfinite(val): return float(val)
    return np.inf
```

```
def _normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s
```
If a.size ==0, returns ones(0) sum 0? But propose n fixed. Fine.

```
def _block_candidate(n, lfrac, rfrac, h_left, h_mid, h_right):
    a=np.zeros(n)
    l=int(round(n*lfrac)); r=int(round(n*rfrac)); m=n-l-r
    if l>0: a[:l]=h_left
    if m>0: a[l:l+m]=h_mid
    if r>0: a[-r:]=h_right
    return _normalize_array(a)
```

Now, code for `_random_log_candidate` with basis:
```
def _random_log_candidate(n, rng, basis, scale=1.0):
    loga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in basis:
        loga += float(rng.normal(0.0, scale)) * p
    loga += float(rng.normal(0.0, scale))
    loga = np.clip(loga, -80.0, 80.0)
    return _from_loga(loga)
```
If basis list empty, returns exp noise normalized.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)**2

using:
  * an initial pool of structured and random low-dimensional sequences,
  * a full-length active-subgradient / mirror-descent polishing loop
    with FFT-based convolution and gradient evaluation,
  * restarts, block perturbations, random log-basis perturbations,
  * and occasional resampling at a longer length if time remains.

The objective is scale-invariant, so all sequences are normalized to sum 1.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(m):
    n = 1
    while n < int(m):
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _normalize_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _from_loga(loga):
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    loga = np.nan_to_num(loga, nan=-700.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-700.0)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -700.0, 700.0)
    m = float(loga.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(loga.size, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.exp(loga - m)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(loga.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def _log_update(a, grad, lr):
    return _from_loga(np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) - lr * grad)


def _self_conv_and_fft(a, N):
    n = a.size
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)
    return c[:2 * n - 1], F


def _fast_score(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _self_conv_and_fft(a, N)[0]
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _score_from_conv(c, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _p_from_c(c, top_frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / c.size
    med = float(np.median(c))
    spread = max(1e-300, cmax - med)
    thr = cmax - top_frac * spread
    active = c >= thr
    cnt = int(active.sum())
    if cnt == 0:
        active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        cnt = 1
    p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p[active] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
    return p


def _grad_from_p(a, p, N, Fa):
    n = a.size
    q = p[::-1]
    Fq = np.fft.rfft(q, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Fq * Fa, N)
    return 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def _candidate_power(n, alpha, lam, gamma):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i + 1.0, -float(alpha))
    if abs(float(lam)) > 1e-8 and n > 1:
        x = i / float(n - 1)
        w = 1.0 + float(lam) * x
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
        if abs(float(gamma) - 1.0) > 1e-8:
            w = np.power(w, float(gamma))
        a = a * w
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return _normalize_array(a)


def _candidate_central(n):
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return _normalize_array(a)


def _block_candidate(n, lfrac, rfrac, h_left, h_mid, h_right):
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    l = int(round(n * float(lfrac)))
    r = int(round(n * float(rfrac)))
    l = min(max(l, 0), n)
    r = min(max(r, 0), n - l)
    m = n - l - r
    if l > 0:
        a[:l] = float(h_left)
    if m > 0:
        a[l:l + m] = float(h_mid)
    if r > 0:
        a[-r:] = float(h_right)
    return _normalize_array(a)


def _random_block_candidate(n, rng):
    for _ in range(20):
        l = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.60))
        r = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.60))
        m = float(rng.uniform(0.00, max(0.0, 0.90 - l - r)))
        if l + r + m <= 0.95:
            break
    h_left = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.30, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0]))
    h_mid = float(rng.choice([0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.30, 1.0]))
    h_right = float(rng.choice([0.05, 0.10, 0.30, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0]))
    return _block_candidate(n, l, r, h_left, h_mid, h_right)


def _build_basis(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    eps = 1e-3
    basis = []
    basis.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    basis.append(x.copy())
    basis.append(x * x)
    basis.append((x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5))
    basis.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(x + eps))
    basis.append(1.0 / np.sqrt((1.0 - x) + eps))
    basis.append(np.exp(-x / 0.05))
    basis.append(np.exp(-x / 0.20))
    basis.append(np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / 0.05))
    basis.append(np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / 0.20))
    return basis


def _random_log_candidate(n, rng, basis, scale=1.0):
    loga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for p in basis:
        loga += float(rng.normal(0.0, scale)) * p
    loga += float(rng.normal(0.0, scale))
    loga = np.clip(loga, -80.0, 80.0)
    return _from_loga(loga)


def _generate_initial_candidates(n, rng, basis):
    cands = []

    cands.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    cands.append(_candidate_central(n))
    cands.append(_candidate_power(n, 0.50, 0.0, 1.0))
    cands.append(_candidate_power(n, 0.45, 0.0, 1.0))
    cands.append(_candidate_power(n, 0.55, 0.0, 1.0))
    cands.append(_candidate_power(n, 0.60, 0.0, 1.0))

    for _ in range(45):
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.65))
        lam = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5))
        cands.append(_candidate_power(n, alpha, lam, gamma))

    base = np.power(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0, -0.5)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    for _ in range(60):
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        mod = 1.0 + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
        mod = np.maximum(mod, 1e-6)
        arr = base * mod
        cands.append(_normalize_array(arr))

    for _ in range(140):
        cands.append(_random_block_candidate(n, rng))

    for _ in range(260):
        scale = float(rng.choice([0.5, 1.0, 1.8]))
        cands.append(_random_log_candidate(n, rng, basis, scale))

    try:
        hseq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if hseq.size == n and np.isfinite(hseq).any():
            cands.append(_normalize_array(hseq))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return cands


def _select_top_candidates(cands, n, k=6):
    Nscore = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    scored = []
    for a in cands:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if a.size != n:
            continue
        a = _normalize_array(a)
        if not np.isfinite(a).all():
            continue
        s = _fast_score(a, Nscore)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((float(s), a))

    if not scored:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)], np.inf

    vals = []
    for item in scored:
        vals.append(item[0])
    order = np.argsort(np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64))
    top = [scored[int(i)][1] for i in order[:max(1, k)]]
    return top, float(scored[int(order[0])][0])


def _resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    if a.size == new_n:
        return a
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _normalize_array(b)


def _evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    if np.isfinite(val):
        return float(val)
    return np.inf


def _polish_sequence(a, time_limit, rng, basis):
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 5.0:
        return a, _fast_score(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    c, Fa = _self_conv_and_fft(a, N)
    score = _score_from_conv(c, a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    lr = None
    top_frac = 0.05
    no_accept = 0
    noise_sigma = 0.12
    last_activity = time.monotonic()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    it = 0
    iterations_between_prints = 20000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if score < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = score
            last_activity = time.monotonic()
            no_accept = 0

        restart = False
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            restart = True
        elif time.monotonic() - last_activity > 45.0 and no_accept > 500:
            restart = True

        if restart:
            noise_sigma = min(0.6, noise_sigma * 1.15)
            mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
            if mode == 0:
                loga = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0, noise_sigma, n)
                a = _from_loga(loga)
            elif mode == 1:
                a = best.copy()
                lo = max(1, n // 100)
                hi = max(lo + 1, n // 4)
                width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
                if width < n:
                    start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
                    a[start:start + width] = np.maximum(
                        0.0,
                        a[start:start + width] * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, width))
                    )
            elif mode == 2:
                b = _random_block_candidate(n, rng)
                t = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.55))
                a = (1.0 - t) * best + t * b
            else:
                use_basis = (
                    basis is not None and len(basis) > 0 and
                    np.asarray(basis[0]).shape[0] == n and
                    float(rng.random()) < 0.75
                )
                if use_basis:
                    a = _random_log_candidate(n, rng, basis, 1.2)
                else:
                    b = _random_block_candidate(n, rng)
                    t = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.60))
                    a = (1.0 - t) * best + t * b

            a = _normalize_array(a)
            c, Fa = _self_conv_and_fft(a, N)
            score = _score_from_conv(c, a)
            lr = None
            top_frac = 0.05
            no_accept = 0
            last_activity = time.monotonic()
            it = 0
            continue

        p = _p_from_c(c, top_frac)
        g = _grad_from_p(a, p, N, Fa)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 0.0:
            break

        if lr is None:
            lr = 0.02 / gmax
        lr = float(np.clip(lr, 1e-12 / float(n), 5.0 * float(n)))

        accepted = False
        for _ in range(8):
            a_try = _log_update(a, g, lr)
            c_try, Fa_try = _self_conv_and_fft(a_try, N)
            score_try = _score_from_conv(c_try, a_try)

            if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < score - 1e-12:
                a = a_try
                c = c_try
                Fa = Fa_try
                score = score_try

                if score < best_score:
                    top_frac = max(0.005, top_frac * 0.90)
                else:
                    top_frac = min(0.20, top_frac * 1.05)

                lr = min(lr * 1.05, 5.0 * float(n))
                accepted = True
                no_accept = 0
                break

            lr *= 0.5
            if lr * gmax < 1e-8:
                break

        if not accepted:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept % 1000 == 0:
                top_frac = min(0.5, top_frac * 1.3)

        it += 1
        if it % iterations_between_prints == 0:
            print(f"      polish iter={it} current={score:.9f} best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return the best nonnegative coefficient sequence found within budget."""
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(0.0, budget)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    n = 5000

    if budget <= 25.0:
        a = _candidate_power(n, 0.50, 0.0, 1.0)
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget - 20.0)

    basis = _build_basis(n)
    inits = _generate_initial_candidates(n, rng, basis)
    top, first_fast = _select_top_candidates(inits, n, k=6)

    # Official best, with safe fallbacks.
    best_official_a = top[0].copy()
    best_official_score = _evaluate_safe(best_official_a)
    best_fast_a = top[0].copy()
    best_fast_score = first_fast

    # Evaluate uniform and one-sided candidates officially, just in case.
    for fb in (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n),
               _candidate_power(n, 0.50, 0.0, 1.0)):
        val = _evaluate_safe(fb)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_official_score:
            best_official_score = val
            best_official_a = fb.copy()

    print(f"Initial best official={best_official_score:.9f} fast={best_fast_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    fracs = [0.50, 0.22, 0.14, 0.10, 0.04]
    for idx, seed_arr in enumerate(top):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 35.0 >= deadline:
            break
        time_left = max(0.0, deadline - now - 20.0)
        if time_left <= 30.0:
            break
        alloc = max(30.0, fracs[min(idx, len(fracs) - 1)] * time_left)
        print(f"Polishing candidate {idx} (fast={ _fast_score(seed_arr, _next_pow2(2*n-1)):.9f}) "
              f"alloc={alloc:.1f}s", flush=True)
        a_best, fast_best = _polish_sequence(seed_arr, alloc, rng, basis)
        if fast_best < best_fast_score:
            best_fast_score = fast_best
            best_fast_a = a_best.copy()
        val = _evaluate_safe(a_best)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_official_score:
            best_official_score = val
            best_official_a = a_best.copy()
        print(f"Done candidate {idx}: official={val:.9f} fast={fast_best:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Optional longer-length refinement if comfortable remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - now - 35.0)
    if time_left > 120.0 and np.isfinite(best_official_score):
        n2 = 6400
        print(f"Trying resampled length {n2} with {time_left:.1f}s remaining", flush=True)
        a2 = _resample_sequence(best_official_a, n2)
        a2, fast2 = _polish_sequence(a2, min(110.0, time_left - 30.0), rng, None)
        val2 = _evaluate_safe(a2)
        if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < best_official_score:
            best_official_score = val2
            best_official_a = a2.copy()
            print(f"Resampled improved official={val2:.9f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"Resampled not improved official={val2:.9f}", flush=True)

    # Final polish on the officially best sequence.
    now = time.monotonic()
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - now - 10.0)
    if time_left > 25.0 and np.isfinite(best_official_score):
        a_fin, fast_fin = _polish_sequence(best_official_a, time_left, rng, None)
        val_fin = _evaluate_safe(a_fin)
        if np.isfinite(val_fin) and val_fin < best_official_score:
            best_official_score = val_fin
            best_official_a = a_fin.copy()
            best_fast_score = min(best_fast_score, fast_fin)
            best_fast_a = a_fin.copy()
        elif fast_fin < best_fast_score:
            best_fast_score = fast_fin
            best_fast_a = a_fin.copy()

    # Fallback: if no official evaluation succeeded, use the best fast sequence.
    if not np.isfinite(best_official_score):
        best_out = best_fast_a
    else:
        best_out = best_official_a

    best_out = _normalize_array(best_out)
    best_out = np.clip(best_out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if np.isfinite(best_official_score):
        print(f"Final official score={best_official_score:.12f}", flush=True)
    else:
        print(f"Final fast score={best_fast_score:.12f}", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in best_out]
```
